## Luckiest animal today – Goat (មមែ) · Great Day

### Picture prompt (square 1:1)
Create a photorealistic hero image of @Goat (the Goat guardian with their goat), the Goat of the Chinese zodiac, as a majestic celestial guardian from a high-budget fantasy epic. The goat symbolizes peace, harmony and a caring heart: a gentle, kind and peaceful expression and posture that show this, mouth calm and closed. It stands on a high terrace of a temple tower inspired by Angkor Wat, with carved apsara reliefs behind it and the moonlit jungle far below, under an enormous luminous full moon and a deep indigo starry sky; moonbeams fall on the goat, golden particles and flowing ribbons of liquid-gold light swirl around it. Cool silver-blue moonlight, warm golden rim light, volumetric mist, cinematic depth of field, ultra-detailed. Square 1:1, full body visible, three-quarter view, centered, with space around it. No text, no letters, no watermark, no logo, no cartoon or plastic look, no cropped body parts.

### Audio design
Music: Gentle magical background music with soft chimes and a light Asian flute. No other voices: the animals do not speak. Keep it well below the voice (about 20 dB quieter) so every word is clear. Sound effects, used lightly: a soft chime when a lucky fact is revealed, a gentle whoosh on scene changes, a warm swell at the ending. No loud or scary sounds.

## Voice-over – 80-second video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Out of all twelve zodiac signs, only one has a Great Day today. Is it yours? Today is a Goat day!

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
5 November belongs to the Goat. Year of the Goat, this Great Day is yours! A Yin Water day!

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
Gentle and kind, the Goat symbolizes peace, harmony, and a caring heart. Let that spirit guide you today.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
Your luck is strong today. Say yes to good chances, and share your happiness. Trust yourself, and act with a kind heart.

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Your lucky color today is green. Wear it, or carry something green with you. Let it remind you to smile and stay confident.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
Your best time is from 5 to 7 in the morning, and your lucky direction is the east. Plan around it.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
Your lucky numbers: 7, 22, and 253. Comment your sign below, and save this video!

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
Come back tomorrow for a brand-new fortune. Follow MyBirthSign, and have a lucky day!

## Voice-over – 180-second video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Born in the Year of the Goat? Here's your lucky color, best time, and numbers for today, plus one gentle reminder.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
Today, Thursday, 5 November 2026, is a Goat day. It is a Great Day for everyone born in the Year of the Goat. This is your day to shine!

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
Gentle and kind, the Goat symbolizes peace, harmony, and a caring heart. Today, this good energy is with you.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
People born in the Year of the Goat are gentle, creative, and compassionate. Goats have a strong artistic sensibility and care deeply for others.

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Born in the Year of the Goat? Your luck is strong today. Stay gentle, kind and peaceful. Say yes to good chances, and share your happiness with the people around you.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
For love and family today, be warm and kind. A kind word, a smile, or a small gift can bring you closer to the people you love.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
For work and study, take one clear step at a time. Finish what matters most first, and ask for help when you need it.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
For money, keep things simple and careful today. Plan before you spend, and save a little for tomorrow.

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
Your lucky color today is green. Wear something green, or carry a small green item with you, to keep good luck close.

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
Your best time today is from 5 to 7 in the morning. It is a good time for important plans, meetings, or a fresh start.

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
Your lucky direction today is the east. When you start something new, face the east, and walk forward with a calm and happy heart.

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
For lucky numbers: 7, 22, and 253. Comment your lucky number below, and let's say it again: 7, 22, 253!

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
The Goat's lucky days of the week are Friday and Sunday. Keep them in mind for plans that matter to you.

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
A gentle reminder: people born in the Year of the Goat can be indecisive or overly dependent on others. Today, stay balanced, and listen to your heart.

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
Make a small wish today, and take one kind step toward it. A peaceful heart helps good luck find its way to you.

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
Before the day ends, take a quiet moment to say thank you. Thank the people who help you, and thank yourself for trying your best.

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
Let's remember today's luck. Lucky color: green. Best time: from 5 to 7 in the morning. Lucky direction: east. Lucky numbers: 7, 22, and 253.

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
That's your Chinese zodiac fortune for today. See all twelve signs and your lucky numbers at mybirthsign dot com. Follow us for tomorrow's fortune. Have a lucky day!

ពេលល្អបំផុតរបស់អ្នកថ្ងៃនេះ គឺម៉ោងប្រាំ ដល់ម៉ោងប្រាំពីរ ព្រឹក។ ជាពេលល្អ សម្រាប់ផែនការសំខាន់ៗ ការជួបជុំ ឬការចាប់ផ្ដើមថ្មី។

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
ទិសសំណាងរបស់អ្នកថ្ងៃនេះ គឺទិសខាងកើត។ ពេលចាប់ផ្ដើមធ្វើអ្វីថ្មី សូមបែរមុខទៅទិសខាងកើត ហើយបោះជំហានទៅមុខ ដោយចិត្តស្ងប់ និងរីករាយ។

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
សម្រាប់លេខសំណាង៖ លេខប្រាំពីរ លេខម្ភៃពីរ និងលេខពីររយហាសិបបី។ សូមសរសេរលេខសំណាងរបស់អ្នក នៅក្នុងមតិយោបល់ ហើយតោះនិយាយម្ដងទៀត៖ ប្រាំពីរ ម្ភៃពីរ ពីររយហាសិបបី!

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
ថ្ងៃសំណាងប្រចាំសប្ដាហ៍ របស់ឆ្នាំមមែ គឺថ្ងៃសុក្រ និងថ្ងៃអាទិត្យ។ សូមចងចាំថ្ងៃទាំងនេះ សម្រាប់ផែនការសំខាន់ៗ របស់អ្នក។

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)

## Long YouTube video – all 12 signs (about 5 minutes)
A long YouTube video (5:10): today's luckiest animal, an overview, then all 12 signs in order (two 10-second clips each), then a short closing. Lucky numbers are given only for today's lucky signs. Add Chanda and the 12 saved characters as ingredients (@Rat, @Ox … @Pig; each sign's clips name its own character). Chanda opens the video on camera and narrates the rest off screen; the animals never speak. Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds. For each scene, press "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice". Put the clips in order 1 to 31.

### Thumbnail prompt – YouTube (16:9)
Make the picture in an image AI (for example Gemini or Flow) with Chanda's picture and the saved zodiac characters (named after their animals) added as references. Upload size: 16:9, at least 640 pixels wide (3840 x 2160 is best), JPG or PNG, under 2 MB if you upload from your phone.

With the words drawn in (English):
Eye-catching YouTube thumbnail, horizontal 16:9, 3840 x 2160. Photorealistic still from a high-budget fantasy epic blending Khmer royal heritage with a Chinese historical fantasy drama: real human faces with natural skin, lifelike zodiac animals in engraved ceremonial gold armor with real metallic reflections, very expressive faces and eyes. Background: a glowing Khmer temple inspired by Angkor Wat under an enormous full moon and a deep indigo starry sky, lotus pool, golden particles. Bright, high-contrast, rich colors (imperial gold, deep navy, ruby red) that stand out on a small phone screen; strong golden rim light separating the characters from the background; slightly blurred background so the faces pop. Big, simple composition with only two or three subjects. Keep the bottom-right corner free (YouTube puts the video length there). No watermark, no logo, no YouTube buttons or arrows, no cartoon or plastic look. The twelve golden-armored zodiac guardians in a tight arc across the frame, all looking at the camera with excited faces; three of them shine much brighter with golden auras, one rests calmly in a soft blue bubble of light. The text sits across the top of the frame in the dark sky, never over a face.

Scene 26 (4:10–4:20)

Scene 28 (4:30–4:40)

Scene 29 (4:40–4:50)

## Story video – YouTube (about 5 minutes)
"The Golden Goat and the Lost Lotus Seed" (ពពែមាស និងគ្រាប់ឈូកដែលបាត់): an original fairy tale (5:00) starring today's luckiest animal. A new story every day: today the golden goat and Sovann the baby elephant must find the lost seed before the full-moon festival. Today's lucky color, direction, best time, lucky numbers and lucky days are part of the story. Make the picture of Sovann once (prompt below) and add it, Chanda and the Goat picture as ingredients in Flow. Chanda opens the story on camera and narrates the rest off screen; Sovann and the animals never speak (their words are told by Chanda). 30 clips of 10 seconds, in order 1 to 30.

### Character prompt – Sovann the baby elephant (make once, keep it)
Create a photorealistic image of Sovann, a tiny, lifelike magical baby elephant with bright, expressive eyes, soft grey-pink skin, big floppy ears and a little golden headpiece, as a small magical companion in a high-budget fantasy epic: realistic anatomy and textures with a soft warm magical glow, sitting on a glowing pink lotus by a still pond at night, a temple inspired by Angkor Wat and an enormous full moon behind, golden dust in the air. Cinematic lighting, beautiful depth of field, ultra-detailed. Square 1:1, full body visible, centered. No text, no letters, no watermark, no cartoon or plastic look.

### YouTube chapters (paste into the description)
0:00 Once upon a time
1:20 The lucky color
1:40 The lucky path
2:30 The stone door
3:40 The full-moon festival
4:30 Today's luck

### Topic and audience
An original animated bedtime story starring the Goat, with today's lucky color, direction and numbers in the plot. For families, children and zodiac fans who enjoy gentle stories.

### Opening hook – 5 options
★ 1. Story question
   English: Can one little golden goat find the lost seed before the full moon rises?
   Khmer: តើពពែមាសតូចមួយ អាចរកគ្រាប់ឈូកដែលបាត់ មុនព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់រះ បានទេ?
2. Mystery
   English: On the night of the full moon, something at the temple went wrong. Only one little hero could fix it.
   Khmer: នៅយប់ព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ មានរឿងមួយមិនប្រក្រតី នៅឯប្រាសាទ។ មានតែវីរបុរសតូចម្នាក់ប៉ុណ្ណោះ ដែលអាចជួយបាន។
3. Identity
   English: Born in the Year of the Goat? This bedtime story is about you.
   Khmer: អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំមមែមែនទេ? រឿងនិទាននេះ គឺនិយាយអំពីអ្នក។
4. Challenge
   English: Three lucky treasures. One hard challenge. One golden goat. Can it make it in time?
   Khmer: រតនសម្បត្តិសំណាងបី។ ឧបសគ្គមួយ។ ពពែមាសមួយ។ តើវាទាន់ពេលឬទេ?

## Voice-over – story video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Can one little golden goat find the lost seed before the full moon rises? Once upon a time, beside an ancient temple in Cambodia, our story begins.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
But this morning, the lotus's magic seed was gone. Without it, the lotus could not bloom, and the festival could not begin.

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
In the middle of the pond, a golden lotus opened, and out came a little golden goat. Today was a Goat day, and the goat felt something special.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
A baby elephant named Sovann came trotting over. "Little Goat," he said, "only you can find the lost seed. Today is your lucky day!"

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Gentle and kind, the Goat symbolizes peace, harmony, and a caring heart. Sovann smiled. That was exactly why the moon had chosen it.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
To bring back the lotus seed, the little hero needed three lucky treasures: its lucky color, its lucky path, and its lucky numbers.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
"Let's go!" said the goat, and it set off on its adventure, with Sovann right by its side.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
People born in the Year of the Goat are gentle, creative, and compassionate. Goats have a strong artistic sensibility and care deeply for others.

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
The first treasure was waiting in a garden of a thousand lanterns. Only one shone brightest. It was green, the goat's lucky color today.

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
The goat tied a green ribbon around its neck. "Wear your lucky color," said Sovann, "and good luck stays close to you."

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
Next, they came to a crossroads with many paths. One path began to glow. It pointed to the east, the goat's lucky direction.

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
But a wide river blocked the path, with only small stepping stones to cross it. The goat stopped. Sometimes, people born in the Year of the Goat can be indecisive or overly dependent on others.

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
Sovann came close and smiled. "Breathe slowly," he said. "Trust your heart. You are stronger than you think."

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
Stone by stone, the goat hopped across the river. Each stone glowed gold under its feet. On the other side, it laughed with joy.

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
The little hero learned that its worries were small, and its good heart was big. Balance and courage always help us grow.

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
At last, they reached a great stone door in the temple. Carved above it were bells, stairs, and a riddle about lucky numbers.

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
"The door opens only at your best time," said Sovann. For the Goat today, that is from 5 to 7 in the morning. They waited patiently.

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
When the time came, the goat rang the golden bells 7 times, its first lucky number. Ding! The door began to shine.

Scene 19 (3:00–3:10)
The door opened, and a stairway of light appeared. The goat climbed 22 glowing steps, its second lucky number, up to the top of the tower.

Scene 20 (3:10–3:20)
At the top of the tower, they found the magic lotus seed, glowing like a tiny sun. It was the heart of the giant lotus!

Scene 21 (3:20–3:30)
Around the seed, seven little lights twinkled, one for each day of the week. Friday and Sunday shone brightest, the Goat's lucky days.

Scene 22 (3:30–3:40)
Time was running out. Holding the seed carefully, the little hero hurried home, with Sovann right beside it.

Scene 23 (3:40–3:50)
Back at the pond, everyone was waiting. The night was dark, and the full moon was rising. Was it too late?

Scene 24 (3:50–4:00)
The goat placed the seed in the giant lotus. For a moment, nothing happened. Then, whoosh! The lotus opened in a burst of golden light!

Scene 25 (4:00–4:10)
The giant lotus bloomed again. Lanterns rose into the sky, and the full-moon festival began. Everyone cheered for the little golden hero!

Scene 26 (4:10–4:20)
The full moon shone down warmly, as if to say thank you. The goat felt peaceful and proud. It had done its very best.

Scene 27 (4:20–4:30)
If you were born in the Year of the Goat, today is your day too. Like our hero, be brave, be kind, and follow your good heart.

Scene 28 (4:30–4:40)
Let's remember today's luck. Lucky color: green. Best time: from 5 to 7 in the morning. Lucky direction: east. Lucky numbers: 7, 22, and 253.

Scene 29 (4:40–4:50)
And so, under the bright full moon, the little golden hero and Sovann fell fast asleep, dreaming of tomorrow.

Scene 30 (4:50–5:00)
That's your Chinese zodiac fortune for today. See all twelve signs and your lucky numbers at mybirthsign dot com. Follow us for tomorrow's fortune. Have a lucky day!

តើពពែមាសតូចមួយ អាចរកគ្រាប់ឈូកដែលបាត់ មុនព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់រះ បានទេ? កាលពីព្រេងនាយ នៅក្បែរប្រាសាទបុរាណមួយ ក្នុងប្រទេសកម្ពុជា រឿងរបស់យើងចាប់ផ្ដើម។

ម្ដងមួយដុំៗ ពពែលោតឆ្លងទន្លេ។ ដុំថ្មនីមួយៗ ភ្លឺពណ៌មាស នៅក្រោមជើងរបស់វា។ នៅម្ខាងទៀត វាសើចយ៉ាងសប្បាយ។

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
វីរបុរសតូចបានរៀនថា ការព្រួយបារម្ភរបស់វា តូចទេ តែចិត្តល្អរបស់វា ធំណាស់។ តុល្យភាព និងភាពក្លាហាន តែងជួយឲ្យយើងរីកចម្រើន។

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
ទីបំផុត ពួកគេមកដល់ទ្វារថ្មដ៏ធំមួយ នៅក្នុងប្រាសាទ។ នៅខាងលើទ្វារ មានរូបចម្លាក់កណ្ដឹង ជណ្ដើរ និងប្រស្នាអំពីលេខសំណាង។

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
សុវណ្ណនិយាយថា "ទ្វារនេះ បើកតែនៅពេលល្អបំផុតរបស់អ្នកប៉ុណ្ណោះ។" សម្រាប់ពពែថ្ងៃនេះ គឺម៉ោងប្រាំ ដល់ម៉ោងប្រាំពីរ ព្រឹក។ ពួកគេរង់ចាំដោយអត់ធ្មត់។

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
ពេលដល់ម៉ោង ពពែវាយកណ្ដឹងមាស ចំនួនប្រាំពីរ ដង ដែលជាលេខសំណាងទីមួយរបស់វា។ ទីង! ទ្វារចាប់ផ្ដើមភ្លឺ។

Scene 19 (3:00–3:10)
ទ្វារបើក ហើយមានជណ្ដើរពន្លឺមួយ លេចឡើង។ ពពែឡើងជណ្ដើរភ្លឺ ចំនួនម្ភៃពីរ កាំ ដែលជាលេខសំណាងទីពីររបស់វា រហូតដល់កំពូលប្រាង្គ។

Scene 20 (3:10–3:20)
នៅលើកំពូលប្រាង្គ ពួកគេបានរកឃើញគ្រាប់ឈូកវេទមន្ត ភ្លឺដូចព្រះអាទិត្យតូចមួយ។ វាគឺជាបេះដូង នៃផ្កាឈូកដ៏ធំ!

Scene 21 (3:20–3:30)
នៅជុំវិញគ្រាប់ឈូក មានពន្លឺតូចៗប្រាំពីរ ភ្លឺភ្លឹបភ្លែតៗ មួយសម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនីមួយៗក្នុងសប្ដាហ៍។ ថ្ងៃសុក្រ និងថ្ងៃអាទិត្យ ភ្លឺជាងគេ ដែលជាថ្ងៃសំណាងរបស់ឆ្នាំមមែ។

Scene 22 (3:30–3:40)
ពេលវេលាជិតអស់ហើយ។ វីរបុរសតូច កាន់គ្រាប់ឈូកយ៉ាងប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន ហើយប្រញាប់ត្រឡប់ទៅផ្ទះវិញ ដោយមានសុវណ្ណ នៅក្បែរ។

Scene 23 (3:40–3:50)
នៅឯស្រះវិញ អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា កំពុងរង់ចាំ។ យប់ងងឹត ហើយព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ កំពុងរះ។ តើយឺតពេលហើយឬ?

Scene 24 (3:50–4:00)
ពពែដាក់គ្រាប់ឈូក ចូលក្នុងផ្កាឈូកដ៏ធំ។ មួយសន្ទុះ គ្មានអ្វីកើតឡើងទេ។ បន្ទាប់មក វឺត! ផ្កាឈូករីក ដោយពន្លឺមាសចែងចាំង!

Scene 25 (4:00–4:10)
ផ្កាឈូកដ៏ធំ រីកម្ដងទៀត។ គោមហោះឡើងលើមេឃ ហើយពិធីបុណ្យព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ ក៏ចាប់ផ្ដើម។ អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា អបអរសាទរវីរបុរសមាសតូច!

Scene 26 (4:10–4:20)
ព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ បញ្ចាំងពន្លឺកក់ក្ដៅចុះមក ហាក់ដូចជាពោលពាក្យអរគុណ។ ពពែមានអារម្មណ៍ស្ងប់ និងមានមោទនភាព។ វាបានព្យាយាមអស់ពីសមត្ថភាព។

Scene 27 (4:20–4:30)
បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំមមែ ថ្ងៃនេះ ក៏ជាថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នកដែរ។ ដូចវីរបុរសតូចរបស់យើង សូមក្លាហាន មានចិត្តល្អ ហើយធ្វើតាមចិត្តល្អរបស់អ្នក។

Scene 28 (4:30–4:40)

Scene 30 (4:50–5:00)
នេះគឺជាជោគជតារាសីចិន សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនេះ។ អ្នកអាចមើលរាសីទាំងដប់ពីរ និងលេខសំណាង នៅលើគេហទំព័រ mybirthsign.com។ សូមតាមដានយើង សម្រាប់ជោគជតាថ្ងៃស្អែក។ សូមឲ្យអ្នកមានសំណាងល្អ!

អ្នកខ្លាំងជាងអ្វីដែលអ្នកគិត។

55
00:02:08,868 --> 00:02:09,950
"

56
00:02:10,000 --> 00:02:12,867
ម្ដងមួយដុំៗ ពពែលោតឆ្លងទន្លេ។

57
00:02:12,917 --> 00:02:17,138
ដុំថ្មនីមួយៗ ភ្លឺពណ៌មាស នៅក្រោមជើងរបស់វា។

58
00:02:17,188 --> 00:02:19,950
នៅម្ខាងទៀត វាសើចយ៉ាងសប្បាយ។

59
00:02:20,000 --> 00:02:23,371
វីរបុរសតូចបានរៀនថា ការព្រួយបារម្ភរបស់វា

60
00:02:23,421 --> 00:02:26,003
តូចទេ តែចិត្តល្អរបស់វា ធំណាស់។

61
00:02:26,053 --> 00:02:29,950
តុល្យភាព និងភាពក្លាហាន តែងជួយឲ្យយើងរីកចម្រើន។

62
00:02:30,000 --> 00:02:32,950
ទីបំផុត ពួកគេមកដល់ទ្វារថ្មដ៏ធំមួយ

63
00:02:33,000 --> 00:02:34,314
នៅក្នុងប្រាសាទ។

64
00:02:34,364 --> 00:02:37,223
នៅខាងលើទ្វារ មានរូបចម្លាក់កណ្ដឹង

65
00:02:37,273 --> 00:02:39,950
ជណ្ដើរ និងប្រស្នាអំពីលេខសំណាង។

66
00:02:40,000 --> 00:02:41,775
សុវណ្ណនិយាយថា "ទ្វារនេះ

67
00:02:41,825 --> 00:02:44,553
បើកតែនៅពេលល្អបំផុតរបស់អ្នកប៉ុណ្ណោះ។

68
00:02:44,603 --> 00:02:48,125
" សម្រាប់ពពែថ្ងៃនេះ គឺម៉ោង 5 ដល់ម៉ោង 7 ព្រឹក។

69
00:02:48,175 --> 00:02:49,950
ពួកគេរង់ចាំដោយអត់ធ្មត់។

70
00:02:50,000 --> 00:02:52,746
ពេលដល់ម៉ោង ពពែវាយកណ្ដឹងមាស

71
00:02:52,796 --> 00:02:56,617
ចំនួន 7 ដង ដែលជាលេខសំណាងទីមួយរបស់វា។

72
00:02:56,667 --> 00:02:57,907
ទីង!

73
00:02:57,957 --> 00:02:59,950
ទ្វារចាប់ផ្ដើមភ្លឺ។

74
00:03:00,000 --> 00:03:03,283
ទ្វារបើក ហើយមានជណ្ដើរពន្លឺមួយ លេចឡើង។

75
00:03:03,333 --> 00:03:05,896
ពពែឡើងជណ្ដើរភ្លឺ ចំនួន 22 កាំ

76
00:03:05,946 --> 00:03:09,950
ដែលជាលេខសំណាងទីពីររបស់វា រហូតដល់កំពូលប្រាង្គ។

77
00:03:10,000 --> 00:03:14,644
នៅលើកំពូលប្រាង្គ ពួកគេបានរកឃើញគ្រាប់ឈូកវេទមន្ត

78
00:03:14,694 --> 00:03:17,195
ភ្លឺដូចព្រះអាទិត្យតូចមួយ។

79
00:03:17,245 --> 00:03:19,950
វាគឺជាបេះដូង នៃផ្កាឈូកដ៏ធំ!

80
00:03:20,000 --> 00:03:21,106
នៅជុំវិញគ្រាប់ឈូក

81
00:03:21,156 --> 00:03:23,555
មានពន្លឺតូចៗប្រាំពីរ ភ្លឺភ្លឹបភ្លែតៗ

82
00:03:23,605 --> 00:03:25,800
មួយសម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនីមួយៗក្នុងសប្ដាហ៍។

83
00:03:25,850 --> 00:03:28,113
ថ្ងៃសុក្រ និងថ្ងៃអាទិត្យ ភ្លឺជាងគេ

84
00:03:28,163 --> 00:03:29,950
ដែលជាថ្ងៃសំណាងរបស់ឆ្នាំមមែ។

85
00:03:30,000 --> 00:03:31,585
ពេលវេលាជិតអស់ហើយ។

86
00:03:31,635 --> 00:03:35,527
វីរបុរសតូច កាន់គ្រាប់ឈូកយ៉ាងប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន

87
00:03:35,577 --> 00:03:37,931
ហើយប្រញាប់ត្រឡប់ទៅផ្ទះវិញ

88
00:03:37,981 --> 00:03:39,950
ដោយមានសុវណ្ណ នៅក្បែរ។

89
00:03:40,000 --> 00:03:44,485
នៅឯស្រះវិញ អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា កំពុងរង់ចាំ។

90
00:03:44,535 --> 00:03:48,438
យប់ងងឹត ហើយព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ កំពុងរះ។

91
00:03:48,488 --> 00:03:49,950
តើយឺតពេលហើយឬ?

92
00:03:50,000 --> 00:03:53,344
ពពែដាក់គ្រាប់ឈូក ចូលក្នុងផ្កាឈូកដ៏ធំ។

93
00:03:53,394 --> 00:03:55,913
មួយសន្ទុះ គ្មានអ្វីកើតឡើងទេ។

94
00:03:55,963 --> 00:03:57,198
បន្ទាប់មក វឺត!

95
00:03:57,248 --> 00:03:59,950
ផ្កាឈូករីក ដោយពន្លឺមាសចែងចាំង!

96
00:04:00,000 --> 00:04:01,968
ផ្កាឈូកដ៏ធំ រីកម្ដងទៀត។

97
00:04:02,018 --> 00:04:03,196
គោមហោះឡើងលើមេឃ

98
00:04:03,246 --> 00:04:06,617
ហើយពិធីបុណ្យព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ ក៏ចាប់ផ្ដើម។

99
00:04:06,667 --> 00:04:09,950
អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា អបអរសាទរវីរបុរសមាសតូច!

100
00:04:10,000 --> 00:04:13,147
ព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ បញ្ចាំងពន្លឺកក់ក្ដៅចុះមក

101
00:04:13,197 --> 00:04:15,032
ហាក់ដូចជាពោលពាក្យអរគុណ។

102
00:04:15,082 --> 00:04:17,819
ពពែមានអារម្មណ៍ស្ងប់ និងមានមោទនភាព។

103
00:04:17,869 --> 00:04:19,950
វាបានព្យាយាមអស់ពីសមត្ថភាព។

104
00:04:20,000 --> 00:04:23,916
បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំមមែ ថ្ងៃនេះ ក៏ជាថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នកដែរ។

105
00:04:23,966 --> 00:04:26,588
ដូចវីរបុរសតូចរបស់យើង សូមក្លាហាន

106
00:04:26,638 --> 00:04:29,950
មានចិត្តល្អ ហើយធ្វើតាមចិត្តល្អរបស់អ្នក។

107
00:04:30,000 --> 00:04:31,644
សូមចងចាំសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។

108
00:04:31,694 --> 00:04:33,095
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌បៃតង។

109
00:04:33,145 --> 00:04:35,595
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 5 ដល់ម៉ោង 7 ព្រឹក។

110
00:04:35,645 --> 00:04:37,208
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសខាងកើត។

111
00:04:37,258 --> 00:04:39,950
លេខសំណាង៖ លេខ 7 លេខ 22 និងលេខ 253។

112
00:04:40,000 --> 00:04:44,016
ហើយដូច្នេះ នៅក្រោមពន្លឺព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់

113
00:04:44,066 --> 00:04:46,543
វីរបុរសមាសតូច និងសុវណ្ណ

114
00:04:46,593 --> 00:04:49,950
ក៏លង់លក់ ដោយសុបិនអំពីថ្ងៃស្អែក។

115
00:04:50,000 --> 00:04:52,258
នេះគឺជាជោគជតារាសីចិន សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនេះ។

116
00:04:52,308 --> 00:04:54,373
អ្នកអាចមើលរាសីទាំង 12 និងលេខសំណាង

117
00:04:54,423 --> 00:04:56,232
នៅលើគេហទំព័រ mybirthsign.com។

118
00:04:56,282 --> 00:04:58,540
សូមតាមដានយើង សម្រាប់ជោគជតាថ្ងៃស្អែក។

119
00:04:58,590 --> 00:04:59,950
សូមឲ្យអ្នកមានសំណាងល្អ!
```

### Subtitles – English (2026-11-05-story.en.srt)
```
1
00:00:00,000 --> 00:00:02,728
Can one little golden goat find the lost

2
00:00:02,778 --> 00:00:04,950
seed before the full moon rises?

3
00:00:05,000 --> 00:00:07,867
Once upon a time, beside an ancient temple

4
00:00:07,917 --> 00:00:09,950
in Cambodia, our story begins.

5
00:00:10,000 --> 00:00:11,306
But this morning

6
00:00:11,356 --> 00:00:14,018
the lotus's magic seed was gone.

7
00:00:14,068 --> 00:00:17,153
Without it, the lotus could not bloom

8
00:00:17,203 --> 00:00:19,950
and the festival could not begin.

9
00:00:20,000 --> 00:00:22,900
In the middle of the pond, a golden lotus

10
00:00:22,950 --> 00:00:25,921
opened, and out came a little golden goat.

11
00:00:25,971 --> 00:00:27,360
Today was a Goat day

12
00:00:27,410 --> 00:00:29,950
and the goat felt something special.

13
00:00:30,000 --> 00:00:31,604
A baby elephant named

14
00:00:31,654 --> 00:00:33,651
Sovann came trotting over.

15
00:00:33,701 --> 00:00:35,383
"Little Goat," he said

16
00:00:35,433 --> 00:00:37,981
"only you can find the lost seed.

17
00:00:38,031 --> 00:00:39,950
Today is your lucky day!"

18
00:00:40,000 --> 00:00:42,763
Gentle and kind, the Goat symbolizes

19
00:00:42,813 --> 00:00:45,497
peace, harmony, and a caring heart.

20
00:00:45,547 --> 00:00:46,591
Sovann smiled.

21
00:00:46,641 --> 00:00:48,153
That was exactly why

22
00:00:48,203 --> 00:00:49,950
the moon had chosen it.

23
00:00:50,000 --> 00:00:52,172
To bring back the lotus seed

24
00:00:52,222 --> 00:00:54,394
the little hero needed three

25
00:00:54,444 --> 00:00:56,934
lucky treasures: its lucky color

26
00:00:56,984 --> 00:00:59,950
its lucky path, and its lucky numbers.

27
00:01:00,000 --> 00:01:02,857
"Let's go!" said the goat

28
00:01:02,907 --> 00:01:06,462
and it set off on its adventure

29
00:01:06,512 --> 00:01:09,950
with Sovann right by its side.

30
00:01:10,000 --> 00:01:12,415
People born in the Year of the Goat

31
00:01:12,465 --> 00:01:15,232
are gentle, creative, and compassionate.

32
00:01:15,282 --> 00:01:18,049
Goats have a strong artistic sensibility

33
00:01:18,099 --> 00:01:19,950
and care deeply for others.

34
00:01:20,000 --> 00:01:22,223
The first treasure was waiting

35
00:01:22,273 --> 00:01:24,874
in a garden of a thousand lanterns.

36
00:01:24,924 --> 00:01:26,768
Only one shone brightest.

37
00:01:26,818 --> 00:01:28,586
It was green, the goat's

38
00:01:28,636 --> 00:01:29,950
lucky color today.

39
00:01:30,000 --> 00:01:31,776
The goat tied a green

40
00:01:31,826 --> 00:01:33,776
ribbon around its neck.

41
00:01:33,826 --> 00:01:36,907
"Wear your lucky color," said Sovann

42
00:01:36,957 --> 00:01:39,950
"and good luck stays close to you."

43
00:01:40,000 --> 00:01:42,555
Next, they came to a crossroads

44
00:01:42,605 --> 00:01:43,900
with many paths.

45
00:01:43,950 --> 00:01:45,832
One path began to glow.

46
00:01:45,882 --> 00:01:47,681
It pointed to the east

47
00:01:47,731 --> 00:01:49,950
the goat's lucky direction.

48
00:01:50,000 --> 00:01:51,724
But a wide river blocked the path

49
00:01:51,774 --> 00:01:53,015
with only small stepping

50
00:01:53,065 --> 00:01:54,036
stones to cross it.

51
00:01:54,086 --> 00:01:54,950
The goat stopped.

52
00:01:55,000 --> 00:01:56,133
Sometimes, people born

53
00:01:56,183 --> 00:01:58,337
in the Year of the Goat can be indecisive

54
00:01:58,387 --> 00:01:59,950
or overly dependent on others.

55
00:02:00,000 --> 00:02:02,686
Sovann came close and smiled.

56
00:02:02,736 --> 00:02:05,139
"Breathe slowly," he said.

57
00:02:05,189 --> 00:02:06,837
"Trust your heart.

58
00:02:06,887 --> 00:02:09,950
You are stronger than you think."

59
00:02:10,000 --> 00:02:11,870
Stone by stone, the goat

60
00:02:11,920 --> 00:02:13,790
hopped across the river.

61
00:02:13,840 --> 00:02:16,830
Each stone glowed gold under its feet.

62
00:02:16,880 --> 00:02:19,950
On the other side, it laughed with joy.

63
00:02:20,000 --> 00:02:23,311
The little hero learned that its worries

64
00:02:23,361 --> 00:02:26,589
were small, and its good heart was big.

65
00:02:26,639 --> 00:02:29,950
Balance and courage always help us grow.

66
00:02:30,000 --> 00:02:32,347
At last, they reached a great

67
00:02:32,397 --> 00:02:34,413
stone door in the temple.

68
00:02:34,463 --> 00:02:36,562
Carved above it were bells

69
00:02:36,612 --> 00:02:39,950
stairs, and a riddle about lucky numbers.

70
00:02:40,000 --> 00:02:41,525
"The door opens only

71
00:02:41,575 --> 00:02:44,044
at your best time," said Sovann.

72
00:02:44,094 --> 00:02:45,462
For the Goat today

73
00:02:45,512 --> 00:02:48,218
that is from 5 to 7 in the morning.

74
00:02:48,268 --> 00:02:49,950
They waited patiently.

75
00:02:50,000 --> 00:02:51,515
When the time came

76
00:02:51,565 --> 00:02:54,820
the goat rang the golden bells 7 times

77
00:02:54,870 --> 00:02:56,820
its first lucky number.

78
00:02:56,870 --> 00:02:57,863
Ding!

79
00:02:57,913 --> 00:02:59,950
The door began to shine.

80
00:03:00,000 --> 00:03:01,095
The door opened

81
00:03:01,145 --> 00:03:03,614
and a stairway of light appeared.

82
00:03:03,664 --> 00:03:06,133
The goat climbed 22 glowing steps

83
00:03:06,183 --> 00:03:07,889
its second lucky number

84
00:03:07,939 --> 00:03:09,950
up to the top of the tower.

85
00:03:10,000 --> 00:03:11,968
At the top of the tower

86
00:03:12,018 --> 00:03:14,687
they found the magic lotus seed

87
00:03:14,737 --> 00:03:16,792
glowing like a tiny sun.

88
00:03:16,842 --> 00:03:19,950
It was the heart of the giant lotus!

89
00:03:20,000 --> 00:03:22,719
Around the seed, seven little lights

90
00:03:22,769 --> 00:03:25,719
twinkled, one for each day of the week.

91
00:03:25,769 --> 00:03:28,258
Friday and Sunday shone brightest

92
00:03:28,308 --> 00:03:29,950
the Goat's lucky days.

93
00:03:30,000 --> 00:03:31,989
Time was running out.

94
00:03:32,039 --> 00:03:34,513
Holding the seed carefully

95
00:03:34,563 --> 00:03:37,232
the little hero hurried home

96
00:03:37,282 --> 00:03:39,950
with Sovann right beside it.

97
00:03:40,000 --> 00:03:43,774
Back at the pond, everyone was waiting.

98
00:03:43,824 --> 00:03:45,538
The night was dark

99
00:03:45,588 --> 00:03:48,381
and the full moon was rising.

100
00:03:48,431 --> 00:03:49,950
Was it too late?

101
00:03:50,000 --> 00:03:51,796
The goat placed the seed

102
00:03:51,846 --> 00:03:53,258
in the giant lotus.

103
00:03:53,308 --> 00:03:55,642
For a moment, nothing happened.

104
00:03:55,692 --> 00:03:56,642
Then, whoosh!

105
00:03:56,692 --> 00:03:57,873
The lotus opened

106
00:03:57,923 --> 00:03:59,950
in a burst of golden light!

107
00:04:00,000 --> 00:04:02,223
The giant lotus bloomed again.

108
00:04:02,273 --> 00:04:04,192
Lanterns rose into the sky

109
00:04:04,242 --> 00:04:06,692
and the full-moon festival began.

110
00:04:06,742 --> 00:04:07,905
Everyone cheered

111
00:04:07,955 --> 00:04:09,950
for the little golden hero!

112
00:04:10,000 --> 00:04:12,693
The full moon shone down warmly

113
00:04:12,743 --> 00:04:14,729
as if to say thank you.

114
00:04:14,779 --> 00:04:17,649
The goat felt peaceful and proud.

115
00:04:17,699 --> 00:04:19,950
It had done its very best.

116
00:04:20,000 --> 00:04:23,256
If you were born in the Year of the Goat

117
00:04:23,306 --> 00:04:25,074
today is your day too.

118
00:04:25,124 --> 00:04:27,719
Like our hero, be brave, be kind

119
00:04:27,769 --> 00:04:29,950
and follow your good heart.

120
00:04:30,000 --> 00:04:31,994
Let's remember today's luck.

121
00:04:32,044 --> 00:04:33,381
Lucky color: green.

122
00:04:33,431 --> 00:04:36,154
Best time: from 5 to 7 in the morning.

123
00:04:36,204 --> 00:04:37,760
Lucky direction: east.

124
00:04:37,810 --> 00:04:39,950
Lucky numbers: 7, 22, and 253.

125
00:04:40,000 --> 00:04:43,158
And so, under the bright full moon

126
00:04:43,208 --> 00:04:46,742
the little golden hero and Sovann fell

127
00:04:46,792 --> 00:04:49,950
fast asleep, dreaming of tomorrow.

128
00:04:50,000 --> 00:04:51,617
That's your Chinese zodiac

129
00:04:51,667 --> 00:04:52,771
fortune for today.

130
00:04:52,821 --> 00:04:54,053
See all twelve signs

131
00:04:54,103 --> 00:04:56,745
and your lucky numbers at mybirthsign.com.

132
00:04:56,795 --> 00:04:58,860
Follow us for tomorrow's fortune.

133
00:04:58,910 --> 00:04:59,950
Have a lucky day!

## Take-it-easy video – the unlucky sign, Ox (8 scenes, 80 seconds)
An 80-second video for the Ox, today's sign to take it easy (in the traditional day cycle the Ox clashes with the Goat day): 8 clips of 10 seconds. Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds and add Chanda and the saved character @Ox as ingredients (the Ox is the only zodiac character in this video, from the first scene to the last). Chanda is on camera and speaks with lip-sync in scenes 1 and 8; in the other scenes she narrates off screen. The animals never speak or move their mouths: they only act. It is one continuous little story in one place (the ox hurries, wobbles, stops to breathe, a little glowing lotus keeps it company, it climbs the temple steps and looks out with hope), so make the clips in order and keep the same setting and light. No lucky numbers or colors in this video: it is about encouragement. Keep it gentle and realistic: a quiet day, not bad luck. For each scene, press "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice". Put the clips in order 1 to 8.

### Opening hook – 5 options
1. Stop the scroll
   English: Stop scrolling! Born in the Year of the Ox? Your luck isn't good today. But don't worry, watch to the end.
   Khmer: ឈប់! កុំអូសរំលង។ អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ រាសីអ្នកថ្ងៃនេះមិនល្អទេ។ តែកុំបារម្ភ សូមមើលឲ្យចប់។
2. Warning
   English: Year of the Ox, wait! Today is a clash day for you. Here's how to get through it calmly.
   Khmer: អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ ចាំបន្តិច! ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃប៉ះឆុងសម្រាប់អ្នក។ មកមើលវិធីឆ្លងកាត់ថ្ងៃនេះដោយស្ងប់។
3. Question
   English: Does everything feel a bit off today? If you were born in the Year of the Ox, here's why.
   Khmer: ថ្ងៃនេះ អ្វីៗហាក់ដូចជាមិនសូវស្រួលមែនទេ? បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ នេះជាមូលហេតុ។
★ 4. Send it to a friend
   English: Know someone born in the Year of the Ox? Send this to them before they make a big decision today.
   Khmer: ស្គាល់អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំឆ្លូវទេ? ផ្ញើវីដេអូនេះទៅគាត់ មុនពេលគាត់សម្រេចចិត្តរឿងធំថ្ងៃនេះ។
5. Only one sign
   English: Out of all twelve signs, only one should slow down today: the Ox.
   Khmer: ក្នុងចំណោមរាសីទាំង 12 មានតែមួយគត់ ដែលគួរធ្វើអ្វីៗយឺតៗថ្ងៃនេះ គឺឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ។

The ox looks up at the light, relaxes and smiles, then waves goodbye together with Chanda, without speaking, its mouth closed. Lanterns float up over the pond behind them. No other animals. ## Voice-over – take-it-easy video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Know someone born in the Year of the Ox? Send this to them before they make a big decision today.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
Today, 5 November, is a Goat day, opposite the Ox in the zodiac circle. Some days don't go to plan.

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
And that's okay. Everyone has days like this. A quiet day is not a bad day. It is a day to breathe.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
Be careful today: you can be stubborn and slow to adapt to sudden change. Pause before you react.

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Be gentle with the people you love, and with yourself. And lean on a friend you trust.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
At work and with money, take small, careful steps. Finish one thing, then the next. Be kind to yourself.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
Slow is still moving forward. Every quiet day makes you stronger, and brighter days are already on their way.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
Rest well tonight. Tomorrow is a new day, and it could be yours. Follow MyBirthSign for your fortune every day!

### Mid-roll ads
YouTube lets you add ad breaks in the middle of videos that are 8 minutes or longer; this one is 8:40. Put the breaks at chapter starts so they don't cut a sentence. Chapter starts after 2:00: 2:40, 3:30, 4:20, 5:20, 8:00. Two or three breaks are enough; too many make viewers leave.

## Voice-over – full fortune video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
One sign has a Great Day today, two have a Good Day, and one should slow down. Which one are you?

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
Today is a Goat day. Coming up: one Great Day sign, two Good Days, one sign to take it easy, and all twelve.

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
Goat, Rabbit and Pig are lucky today, and the Ox should slow down. Find your sign in the chapters below.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
Before we start, comment your sign below, and subscribe so you get your fortune every day. Keep a calm heart.

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
5 November belongs to the Goat. Year of the Goat, this Great Day is yours! A Yin Water day!

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
Gentle and kind, the Goat symbolizes peace, harmony, and a caring heart. Let that spirit guide you today.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
People born in the Year of the Goat are often described as gentle, creative, and compassionate. A Yin Water day!

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
Your luck is strong today. Say yes to good chances, and share your happiness. Trust yourself, and act with a kind heart.

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
For love today, be warm and kind. A kind word or a small gift brings you closer. Small choices bring big luck.

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
For work and study, take one clear step at a time, and finish what matters most first. Share your luck.

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
For money, keep it simple. Plan before you spend, and save a little for tomorrow. Smile, and share your luck with someone today.

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
Your lucky color today is green. Wear it, or carry something green with you. Let it remind you to smile and stay confident.

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
Your best time is from 5 to 7 in the morning, and your lucky direction is the east. Plan around it.

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
Your lucky numbers: 7, 22, and 253. Comment your sign below, and save this video!

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
The Goat's lucky days of the week are Friday and Sunday. Keep them for plans that matter. A Yin Water day!

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
A gentle reminder: Goat people can be indecisive or overly dependent on others. Stay balanced today. Keep a calm heart.

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
Next, the Year of the Rabbit: a Good Day! The Rabbit shares a lucky triangle with the Goat. Small choices bring big luck.

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
Team up with a friend today. Shared plans go well. Stay with me: more good news is coming.

Scene 19 (3:00–3:10)
Your lucky color is purple, and your best time is from 1 to 3 in the afternoon. A Yin Water day!

Scene 20 (3:10–3:20)
Your lucky direction is the southwest. Lucky numbers: 6, 45, and 369. Small good choices add up to a lucky day.

Scene 21 (3:20–3:30)
Rabbit friends, stay close to the Goat people today. Good luck grows when you share it. Small choices bring big luck.

Scene 22 (3:30–3:40)
Next, the Year of the Pig: a Good Day! The Pig shares a lucky triangle with the Goat. Share your luck.

Scene 23 (3:40–3:50)
A good day to meet people and start something nice. Keep smiling. Stay with me: more good news is coming.

Scene 24 (3:50–4:00)
Your lucky color is gray, and your best time is from 5 to 7 in the morning. Keep a calm heart.

Scene 25 (4:00–4:10)
Your lucky direction is the east. Lucky numbers: 5, 87, and 253. Small good choices add up to a lucky day.

Scene 26 (4:10–4:20)
Pig friends, stay close to the Goat people today. Good luck grows when you share it. Share your luck.

Scene 27 (4:20–4:30)
Today, 5 November, is a Goat day, opposite the Ox in the zodiac circle. Some days don't go to plan.

Scene 28 (4:30–4:40)
And that's okay. Everyone has days like this. A quiet day is not a bad day. It is a day to breathe.

Scene 29 (4:40–4:50)
Be careful today: you can be stubborn and slow to adapt to sudden change. Pause before you react.

Scene 30 (4:50–5:00)
Be gentle with the people you love, and with yourself. And lean on a friend you trust.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
សម្រាប់ស្នេហា ថ្ងៃនេះ សូមមានចិត្តកក់ក្ដៅ។ ពាក្យល្អ ឬអំណោយតូចមួយ នាំឲ្យកាន់តែជិតស្និទ្ធ។ ជម្រើសតូចៗ នាំសំណាងធំ។

សូមមើលបន្ត មានដំណឹងល្អៗទៀត។

63

ពណ៌សំណាងគឺពណ៌ស្វាយ

64

ហើយពេលល្អបំផុតគឺម៉ោង 1 ដល់ម៉ោង 3 រសៀល។

65
00:03:08,235 --> 00:03:09,950
ថ្ងៃធាតុទឹក!

66

ទិសសំណាងគឺទិសនិរតី។

67
00:03:12,468 --> 00:03:15,405
លេខសំណាង៖ 6 45 និង 369។

68

ជម្រើសល្អៗតូចៗ នាំមកនូវថ្ងៃដ៏សំណាង។

69
00:03:20,000 --> 00:03:22,297
អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំថោះ ថ្ងៃនេះ

70
00:03:22,347 --> 00:03:24,746
សូមនៅជិតអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំមមែ។

71
00:03:24,796 --> 00:03:27,705
សំណាងកាន់តែច្រើន ពេលចែករំលែក។

72
00:03:27,755 --> 00:03:29,950
ជម្រើសតូចៗ នាំសំណាងធំ។

73
00:03:30,000 --> 00:03:32,871
បន្ទាប់ ឆ្នាំកុរ៖ ថ្ងៃល្អ!

74
00:03:32,921 --> 00:03:38,040
ឆ្នាំកុរ ស្ថិតក្នុងត្រីកោណសំណាង ជាមួយឆ្នាំមមែ។

75
00:03:38,090 --> 00:03:39,950
សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

76
00:03:40,000 --> 00:03:42,885
ថ្ងៃនេះ ល្អសម្រាប់ជួបមនុស្ស

77

និងចាប់ផ្ដើមរឿងល្អៗ។

78

សូមរក្សាស្នាមញញឹម។

79
00:03:47,065 --> 00:03:49,950
សូមមើលបន្ត មានដំណឹងល្អៗទៀត។

80
00:03:50,000 --> 00:03:52,418
ពណ៌សំណាងគឺពណ៌ប្រផេះ

81

ហើយពេលល្អបំផុតគឺម៉ោង 5 ដល់ម៉ោង 7 ព្រឹក។

82

ជម្រើសល្អៗតូចៗ នាំមកនូវថ្ងៃដ៏សំណាង។

88

89

សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

90

91

ទល់មុខឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ ក្នុងរង្វង់រាសី។

92

មានថ្ងៃខ្លះ អ្វីៗមិនដូចការគ្រោងទុកទេ។

93

95
00:04:34,298 --> 00:04:37,494
ថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់ មិនមែនជាថ្ងៃអាក្រក់ទេ

96

សូមប្រយ័ត្នថ្ងៃនេះ៖ ជួនកាលរឹងរូស

98

99

101

104

105

111

113

116

ហើយបញ្ចប់អ្វីដែលអ្នកបានចាប់ផ្ដើម។

## Keep the voice the same in every scene
Flow makes the voice fresh for every clip, so the voice text in every scene is written to describe exactly the same voice: the same words, in the same order, at the start of the audio line, for every scene and every video. To keep it that way:
- Always use "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice". Do not retype, shorten or translate the voice text, and do not add other words about the voice.
- Make all clips of one video in the same Flow project, with the same model and settings for every clip.
- Use the same language for the whole video (all English or all Khmer).
- Listen to each clip as it is made. If one sounds different, make only that clip again with the same copied prompt.

## Khmer voice – how to get clear Khmer
Every Khmer voice line in this pack is written for speaking: short sentences, a ។ at each pause, and numbers written out in Khmer words (for example ហុកសិបបួន, not 64) so the voice does not read them in English. Subtitles and captions keep the digits.

### If you use Google Flow
Use "Copy + Khmer voice" for each scene; the voice description is already included. Flow's Khmer can be unclear on some clips. Listen to every clip, and remake any clip where words are wrong, cut off or English-sounding. If Khmer stays unclear, make the clips without voice ("Copy") and add a recorded Khmer voice-over instead.

### If you use a text-to-speech app
- Language: Cambodian Khmer (km-KH). Pick a real Khmer voice, for example Microsoft Azure's female voice "km-KH-SreymomNeural" (used by many TTS apps and sites). Do not use an English voice with Khmer text.
- Speed: 0.85× to 0.9×. Pitch: natural. Pause between sentences: normal.
- Paste the Khmer voice-over text from this pack exactly as it is (Khmer script, no romanization).
- Make one scene at a time if the app cuts off long text, then join the clips in your editor.
- Before using a new voice, test it with these two sentences and listen carefully:
  សួស្តីអ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា! សូមស្វាគមន៍មកកាន់ MyBirthSign.com។
  ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំខ្លា។ សូមជឿជាក់លើខ្លួនឯង ហើយកុំភ្លេចអត់ធ្មត់។
  Check that every word is clear Cambodian Khmer, sentence endings are complete, and speed and volume stay even.

### Record it yourself
A native speaker reading the Khmer voice-over slowly and clearly is always the most natural choice.

## Style (paste first in every scene)
Vertical 9:16. Photorealistic live-action fantasy film, the look of a high-budget fantasy epic that blends Khmer royal heritage with the grandeur of a Chinese historical fantasy drama. Real human faces with natural skin texture, the zodiac characters saved in Flow, each named after its animal (@Rat, @Ox, @Tiger, @Rabbit, @Dragon, @Snake, @Horse, @Goat, @Monkey, @Rooster, @Dog, @Pig): a guardian warrior in ornate armor themed on the animal, with a lifelike animal beside them (realistic anatomy, real fur, feathers or scales), always exactly as in the saved character picture, noble and powerful (never cute toys or cartoons). Setting: a majestic Khmer temple inspired by Angkor Wat with towering carved stone, an enormous luminous full moon, deep indigo starry sky, drifting mist, a still reflecting pool with floating pink lotus flowers and ornate golden lanterns. A living, magical sky in every shot: thin silver clouds drift slowly across the moon, stars twinkle, a soft halo pulses around the moon and sky lanterns rise gently in the distance. Action and magic in the style of a Chinese fantasy (xianxia) drama: graceful wire-fu flying leaps, slow-motion martial-arts moves, flowing robes and capes, glowing sword energy and swirling qi light, always without blood, injury or fighting between characters. Golden magic: shimmering gold particles, flowing ribbons of liquid-gold light and floating gold dust that never hide a face. Physically real lighting: cool silver-blue moonlight, warm golden rim light, soft lantern glow, volumetric light rays; colors deep navy, imperial gold, ruby red and lotus pink. Cinematic depth of field, smooth slow camera movement, high-end visual effects, 4K detail. The zodiac guardians and their animals never talk and never move their mouths; only Chanda, the presenter, speaks. No text, no letters, no subtitles, no watermark, no logos, no cartoon or plastic look, no low-quality CGI.

## Scene 1 – Goat day (Chanda on camera)
@Chanda, a beautiful Cambodian woman in her early twenties with a warm, kind and confident presence: oval face, soft defined cheekbones, warm amber-brown almond eyes, natural brows, rosy cheeks, natural skin with real texture and refined natural makeup, glossy black hair swept into a low side bun on her right side held by an elaborate golden lotus hairpin with fine dangling gold chains, delicate gold flower earrings, a fitted cream-and-gold Khmer silk brocade blouse with a high mandarin collar and long sleeves, a large round gold medallion on a chain engraved with all twelve zodiac animals, an ornate gold belt with a single ruby-red gemstone at the buckle, a deep navy silk sampot to the ankles with a vertical gold-embroidered front panel and a wide band of Khmer gold patterns at the hem, and gold bangles on both wrists, stands in front of the Angkor-style temple at night, facing the camera and speaking to the viewer with natural lip movements, then turns and gestures toward the pond. Chanda is the saved Flow character @Chanda. Then: magical and powerful. @Goat (the Goat guardian with their goat) stands on its glowing lotus pedestal on the pond in front of the Angkor-style temple under a huge full moon. Its eyes light up, a golden aura bursts around it, and it lowers its curled horns playfully as its guardian spins a glowing jade staff, and every turn sends rings of soft green and gold light across the terrace, as today's Water magic swirls around them: flowing ribbons of clear blue water, rippling rings of light and cool silver-blue mist. The lotus pond ripples with golden light, and sky lanterns drift up behind the temple. Dynamic camera: start close on its face, then sweep up and follow it through the air. Cute, majestic and full of energy, matching the reference image.

## Scene 2 – Lucky signs
Magical and powerful. @Goat (the Goat guardian with their goat), @Rabbit (the Rabbit guardian with their rabbit) and @Pig (the Pig guardian with their pig) fly in from three directions on trails of golden light and land together on glowing lotus pedestals in front of the temple. As they land, a rain of tiny golden stars falls gently over the pond. They look at each other and cheer. The camera orbits around them in a smooth circle. The animals match the reference images.

## Scene 3 – Ox, take it easy
Photorealistic golden fantasy-epic style, same saved Flow characters (Chanda and the zodiac characters named after their animals). @Ox (the Ox guardian with their ox) rests on a lotus pedestal by the pond: it naps peacefully under a big lotus leaf held up like an umbrella, and the full moon shines calmly on the still water. The mood is peaceful, safe and magical, not sad. The camera moves in slowly. The ox is the saved Flow character @Ox.

## Scene 4 – Outro
Magical and powerful. Thousands of fireflies rise from the pond and gather into a huge glowing lotus flower in the sky, and the huge full moon glows over the pond. The camera rises up and pulls back for a wide, epic view, leaving open sky at the top of the frame for a title.

## Scene 5 – Lucky numbers (about 10–12 seconds)
Magical and powerful. A huge glowing golden zodiac wheel rises out of the lotus pond in front of the temple, with the twelve golden-armored zodiac guardians around its rim. It spins faster and faster, shooting beams of golden light and sparkles, then slows to a gentle, shining turn under the full moon. The camera slowly pushes in, keeping open space in the middle and lower part of the frame for the number captions. (Loop the slow-turning part, or slow-zoom a still picture, to fill the time.)
